#include "ti_msp_dl_config.h"
#include "tjc_usart_hmi.h"
#include "stdio.h"

#define FRAME_LENGTH 7
volatile uint32_t delay_times = 0;
volatile uint8_t uart_data = 0;
volatile uint32_t now_time = 0;

//                         ms
void delay_ms(unsigned int ms)
{
   delay_times = ms;
   while (delay_times != 0)
      ;
}

int main(void)
{
   SYSCFG_DL_init();
   //
   NVIC_ClearPendingIRQ(UART_0_INST_INT_IRQN);
   //
   NVIC_EnableIRQ(UART_0_INST_INT_IRQN);
   int a = 100;
   char str[100];
   uint32_t last_time = 0;
   while (1)
   {
      if (now_time - last_time >= 1000)
                                                                                (   )


                                           310
                                                                                        (   )
     {
         last_time = now_time;
         sprintf(str, "n0.val=%d", a);
         tjc_send_string(str);
         sprintf(str, "t0.txt=\"%d\"\xff\xff\xff", a);
         tjc_send_string(str);
         sprintf(str, "click b0,1\xff\xff\xff");
         tjc_send_string(str);
         delay_ms(50);
         sprintf(str, "click b0,0\xff\xff\xff");
         tjc_send_string(str);

        a++;
     }
     // stm32f103     GND                                GND,
     // stm32f103     TX1(PA9)                                  RX
     // stm32f103     RX1(PA10)                                  TX
     // stm32f103     5V                   5V,                   ,    5V

     //
     //                      7
     //                  1             2             3
     // 0x55      1              1               1         0xffffff
     //          01
     //                  1             2        3
     // 0x55        01       led           led        0xffffff
     //      1                       printh 55 01 01 00 ff ff ff           1   led
     //      2                       printh 55 01 04 01 ff ff ff           4   led
     //      3                       printh 55 01 00 01 ff ff ff           0   led
     //      4                       printh 55 01 04 00 ff ff ff           4   led

     //          02    03
     //                 1               2       3
     // 0x55        02/03                    00    0xffffff
     //      1                       printh 55 02 64 00 ff ff ff           h0.val=100
     //      2                       printh 55 02 00 00 ff ff ff           h0.val=0
     //      3                       printh 55 03 64 00 ff ff ff           h1.val=100
     //      4                       printh 55 03 00 00 ff ff ff           h1.val=0

      //
      while (usize >= FRAME_LENGTH)
      {
         //
         if (usize >= FRAME_LENGTH && u(0) == 0x55 && u(4) == 0xff && u(5) == 0xff &&␣
,→u(6) == 0xff)

         {
            //
            if (u(1) == 0x01)
            {
                sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"led %d is %s\"", u(2), u(3) ? "on" : "off");
                tjc_send_string(str);
                if (u(2) == 0x00)
                {
                   if (u(3) == 0x00)
                   {
                      // DL_GPIO_clearPins(LED1_PORT,LED1_PIN_14_PIN);//
                   }
                   else
                   {
                      // DL_GPIO_setPins(LED1_PORT,LED1_PIN_14_PIN); //
                   }
                                                                                        (   )


                                                         311
                                                                     (   )
                   }
                }
                else if (u(1) == 0x02)
                {
                   //         h0

                   sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"h0.val is %d\"", u(2));
                   tjc_send_string(str);
                }
                else if (u(1) == 0x03)
                {
                   //         h1
                   sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"h1.val is %d\"", u(2));
                   tjc_send_string(str);
                }

               udelete(7); //
            }
            else
            {

                //            1
                udelete(1);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
}

void SysTick_Handler()
{
   if (delay_times != 0)
   {
      delay_times--;
   }
   now_time++;
}

//
void UART_0_INST_IRQHandler(void)
{
   //
   switch (DL_UART_Main_getPendingInterrupt(UART_0_INST))
   {
   case DL_UART_MAIN_IIDX_RX: //
      //
      uart_data = DL_UART_Main_receiveData(UART_0_INST);
      //
      // uart0_send_char(uart_data);
      writeRingBuff(uart_data);
      break;

    default: //
       break;
    }
}

//arduino GND                                           GND,
//arduino 9                                        RX
//arduino 8                                        TX
//arduino 5V           5V,                                     ,            5V
//                                             ,                    8   9
#include <SoftwareSerial.h>
SoftwareSerial TJC(8,9);
#define FRAME_LENGTH 7

int a;
unsigned long nowtime;
void setup() {
  // put your setup code here, to run once:
  //
  TJC.begin(115200);

    //                     88 ff ff ff,
    while (TJC.read() >= 0); //
    TJC.print("page main\xff\xff\xff"); //                                              main
    nowtime = millis(); //
}

void loop() {
  // put your main code here, to run repeatedly:
  char str[100];
  if (millis() >= nowtime + 1000) {
    nowtime = millis(); //

       // sprintf                   n0 val
       sprintf(str, "n0.val=%d\xff\xff\xff", a);
       //
       TJC.print(str);

       // sprintf                                   t0 txt
       sprintf(str, "t0.txt=\"                      %d\"\xff\xff\xff", a);
       //
       TJC.print(str);


                                                                                                   (   )



                                                                    319
                                                                                      (     )
     // sprintf                     b0                    ,
     sprintf(str, "click b0,1\xff\xff\xff");
     //
     TJC.print(str);

     delay(50);   //           50ms,

     // sprintf                     b0                    ,
     sprintf(str, "click b0,0\xff\xff\xff");
     //
     TJC.print(str);

     a++;
 }

 //
 //                    7
 //                1              2        3
 //0x55       1            1           1        0xffffff
 //          01
 //                1              2        3
 //0x55      01        led            led        0xffffff
 //    1                        printh 55 01 01 00 ff ff ff   1   led
 //    2                        printh 55 01 04 01 ff ff ff   4   led
 //    3                        printh 55 01 00 01 ff ff ff   0   led
 //    4                        printh 55 01 04 00 ff ff ff   4   led

 //          02 03
 //              1                2        3
 //0x55      02/03                      00    0xffffff
 //    1                        printh 55 02 64 00 ff ff ff   h0.val=100
 //    2                        printh 55 02 00 00 ff ff ff   h0.val=0
 //    3                        printh 55 03 64 00 ff ff ff   h1.val=100
 //    4                        printh 55 03 00 00 ff ff ff   h1.val=0


  //
  while (TJC.available() >= FRAME_LENGTH) {
    unsigned char ubuffer[FRAME_LENGTH];
    //                1
    unsigned char frame_header = TJC.peek();
    //                    (0x55)
    if (frame_header == 0x55) {
       //                 7
       TJC.readBytes(ubuffer, FRAME_LENGTH);
       if (ubuffer[4] == 0xff && ubuffer[5] == 0xff && ubuffer[6] == 0xff) {
          if(ubuffer[1] == 0x01)
          {
            //        LED
            sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"led %d is %s\"\xff\xff\xff", ubuffer[2], ubuffer[3] ?
,→"on" : "off");

            TJC.print(str);

        }else if(ubuffer[1] == 0x02)
        {
          //              h0.val
          sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"h0.val is %d\"\xff\xff\xff", ubuffer[2]);
          TJC.print(str);

        }else if(ubuffer[1] == 0x03)
        {
                                                                                      (     )


                                                    320
                                                                                   (   )
               //              h1.val
               sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"h1.val is %d\"\xff\xff\xff", ubuffer[2]);
               TJC.print(str);

           }

          }
        } else {
          TJC.read();   //              1
        }
    }
}

//arduino GND                                     GND,
//arduino TX3                                     RX
//arduino RX3                                     TX
//arduino 5V           5V,                               ,         5V
//                                         ,                 TX3    RX3
#define TJC Serial3
#define FRAME_LENGTH 7

int a;
unsigned long nowtime;
void setup() {
  // put your setup code here, to run once:
  //
  TJC.begin(115200);

    //                     88 ff ff ff,
    while (TJC.read() >= 0); //
    TJC.print("page main\xff\xff\xff"); //                                           main
    nowtime = millis(); //
}

void loop() {
  // put your main code here, to run repeatedly:
  char str[100];
  if (millis() >= nowtime + 1000) {
    nowtime = millis(); //

       // sprintf                   n0 val
       sprintf(str, "n0.val=%d\xff\xff\xff", a);
       //
       TJC.print(str);

       // sprintf                              t0 txt
       sprintf(str, "t0.txt=\"                 %d\"\xff\xff\xff", a);
       //
       TJC.print(str);


       // sprintf                     b0                           ,
       sprintf(str, "click b0,1\xff\xff\xff");
       //
       TJC.print(str);
                                                                                                             (        )



                                                             325
                                                                                      (     )


     delay(50);   //           50ms,

     // sprintf                     b0                    ,
     sprintf(str, "click b0,0\xff\xff\xff");
     //
     TJC.print(str);

     a++;
 }

 //
 //                    7
 //                1              2        3
 //0x55       1            1           1        0xffffff
 //          01
 //                1              2        3
 //0x55      01        led            led        0xffffff
 //    1                        printh 55 01 01 00 ff ff ff   1   led
 //    2                        printh 55 01 04 01 ff ff ff   4   led
 //    3                        printh 55 01 00 01 ff ff ff   0   led
 //    4                        printh 55 01 04 00 ff ff ff   4   led

 //          02 03
 //              1                2        3
 //0x55      02/03                      00    0xffffff
 //    1                        printh 55 02 64 00 ff ff ff   h0.val=100
 //    2                        printh 55 02 00 00 ff ff ff   h0.val=0
 //    3                        printh 55 03 64 00 ff ff ff   h1.val=100
 //    4                        printh 55 03 00 00 ff ff ff   h1.val=0


  //
  while (TJC.available() >= FRAME_LENGTH) {
    unsigned char ubuffer[FRAME_LENGTH];
    //                1
    unsigned char frame_header = TJC.peek();
    //                    (0x55)
    if (frame_header == 0x55) {
       //                 7
       TJC.readBytes(ubuffer, FRAME_LENGTH);
       if (ubuffer[4] == 0xff && ubuffer[5] == 0xff && ubuffer[6] == 0xff) {
          if(ubuffer[1] == 0x01)
          {
            //        LED
            sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"led %d is %s\"\xff\xff\xff", ubuffer[2], ubuffer[3] ?
,→"on" : "off");

            TJC.print(str);

        }else if(ubuffer[1] == 0x02)
        {
          //              h0.val
          sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"h0.val is %d\"\xff\xff\xff", ubuffer[2]);
          TJC.print(str);

        }else if(ubuffer[1] == 0x03)
        {
          //              h1.val
          sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"h1.val is %d\"\xff\xff\xff", ubuffer[2]);
          TJC.print(str);

                                                                                      (     )


                                                    326
                                                 (   )
           }

          }
        } else {
          TJC.read();   //   1
        }
    }
}

//arduino IDE             AirM2M CORE ESP32C3
//arduino GND                     GND,
//arduino IO4                     RX
//arduino IO5                     TX
//arduino 5V           5V,              ,            5V
//                        TX_Pin RX_Pin
#define TJC Serial
#define TX_Pin 4
#define RX_Pin 5
#define FRAME_LENGTH 7
#define LED_PIN0 12
#define LED_PIN1 13

int a;
unsigned long nowtime;
void setup() {
  // put your setup code here, to run once:
  //
  TJC.begin(115200, SERIAL_8N1, RX_Pin, TX_Pin);
  //TJC.begin(115200);
  //                     88 ff ff ff,
  while (TJC.read() >= 0); //
  TJC.print("page main\xff\xff\xff"); //                    main
  nowtime = millis(); //

    //      esp32c3      LED
    pinMode(LED_PIN0, OUTPUT);
    pinMode(LED_PIN1, OUTPUT);
    digitalWrite(LED_PIN0, LOW);
    digitalWrite(LED_PIN1, LOW);

}

void loop() {
  // put your main code here, to run repeatedly:
  char str[100];
  if (millis() >= nowtime + 1000) {
    nowtime = millis(); //

      // sprintf                   n0 val
      sprintf(str, "n0.val=%d\xff\xff\xff", a);
      //
      TJC.print(str);

      // sprintf                   t0 txt
      sprintf(str, "t0.txt=\"      %d\"\xff\xff\xff", a);
      //
                                                                      (   )



                                              336
                                                                                      (     )
     TJC.print(str);


     // sprintf                     b0                    ,
     sprintf(str, "click b0,1\xff\xff\xff");
     //
     TJC.print(str);

     delay(50);   //           50ms,

     // sprintf                     b0                    ,
     sprintf(str, "click b0,0\xff\xff\xff");
     //
     TJC.print(str);



     a++;
 }

 //
 //                    7
 //                1              2        3
 //0x55       1            1           1        0xffffff
 //          01
 //                1              2        3
 //0x55      01        led            led        0xffffff
 //    1                        printh 55 01 01 00 ff ff ff   1   led
 //    2                        printh 55 01 04 01 ff ff ff   4   led
 //    3                        printh 55 01 00 01 ff ff ff   0   led
 //    4                        printh 55 01 04 00 ff ff ff   4   led

 //          02 03
 //              1                2        3
 //0x55      02/03                      00    0xffffff
 //    1                        printh 55 02 64 00 ff ff ff   h0.val=100
 //    2                        printh 55 02 00 00 ff ff ff   h0.val=0
 //    3                        printh 55 03 64 00 ff ff ff   h1.val=100
 //    4                        printh 55 03 00 00 ff ff ff   h1.val=0


  //
  while (TJC.available() >= FRAME_LENGTH) {
    unsigned char ubuffer[FRAME_LENGTH];
    //                 1
    unsigned char frame_header = TJC.peek();
    //                     (0x55)
    if (frame_header == 0x55) {
       //                  7
       TJC.readBytes(ubuffer, FRAME_LENGTH);
       if (ubuffer[4] == 0xff && ubuffer[5] == 0xff && ubuffer[6] == 0xff) {
          if(ubuffer[1] == 0x01)
          {
            //         LED
            sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"led %d is %s\"\xff\xff\xff", ubuffer[2], ubuffer[3] ?
,→"on" : "off");

            TJC.print(str);
            if(ubuffer[2]==0x00)
            {
               //    ESP32C3    LED 1
               digitalWrite(LED_PIN0, ubuffer[3]);
                                                                                      (     )


                                                    337
                                                                                 (   )
               }else if(ubuffer[2]==0x01)
               {
                 //    ESP32C3    LED 2
                 digitalWrite(LED_PIN1, ubuffer[3]);
               }

           }else if(ubuffer[1] == 0x02)
           {
             //              h0.val
             sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"h0.val is %d\"\xff\xff\xff", ubuffer[2]);
             TJC.print(str);

           }else if(ubuffer[1] == 0x03)
           {
             //              h1.val
             sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"h1.val is %d\"\xff\xff\xff", ubuffer[2]);
             TJC.print(str);

           }

          }
        } else {
          TJC.read();   //              1
        }
    }
}

 //arduino IDE                       Raspberry Pi Pico - Arduino Mbed OS RP2040 Boards
 //pico GND                               GND,
 //pico IO0                               RX
 //pico IO1                               TX
 //pico 5V                  5V,                 ,      5V

 #define FRAME_LENGTH 7

 unsigned long nowtime;

   //

     //                    88 ff ff ff,

     //            pico      LED

 }

      //
      TJC.print(str);

      delay(50);   //           50ms,

      // sprintf                     b0                   ,
      sprintf(str, "click b0,0\xff\xff\xff");
      //
      TJC.print(str);



      a++;
  }

  //
  //                    7
  //                1               2       3
  //0x55       1            1           1         0xffffff
  //          01
  //                1               2       3
  //0x55      01        led            led        0xffffff
  //    1                        printh 55 01 01 00 ff ff ff   1   led
  //    2                        printh 55 01 04 01 ff ff ff   4   led
  //    3                        printh 55 01 00 01 ff ff ff   0   led
  //    4                        printh 55 01 04 00 ff ff ff   4   led

  //          02 03
  //              1                 2       3
  //0x55      02/03                      00    0xffffff
  //    1                        printh 55 02 64 00 ff ff ff   h0.val=100
  //    2                        printh 55 02 00 00 ff ff ff   h0.val=0
  //    3                        printh 55 03 64 00 ff ff ff   h1.val=100
  //    4                        printh 55 03 00 00 ff ff ff   h1.val=0


   //
   while (TJC.available() >= FRAME_LENGTH) {
     unsigned char ubuffer[FRAME_LENGTH];
     //                 1
     unsigned char frame_header = TJC.peek();
     //                     (0x55)
     if (frame_header == 0x55) {
        //                  7
        TJC.readBytes(ubuffer, FRAME_LENGTH);
        if (ubuffer[4] == 0xff && ubuffer[5] == 0xff && ubuffer[6] == 0xff) {
           if(ubuffer[1] == 0x01)
           {
             //         LED
             sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"led %d is %s\"\xff\xff\xff", ubuffer[2], ubuffer[3] ?
,→"on" : "off");

             TJC.print(str);
             if(ubuffer[2]==0x00)
             {
                //          pico    LED 1
                digitalWrite(LED_BUILTIN, ubuffer[3]);
             }

         }else if(ubuffer[1] == 0x02)
                                                                                      (      )


                                                    361
                                                                                   (   )
           {
               //              h0.val
               sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"h0.val is %d\"\xff\xff\xff", ubuffer[2]);
               TJC.print(str);

           }else if(ubuffer[1] == 0x03)
           {
             //              h1.val
             sprintf(str, "msg.txt=\"h1.val is %d\"\xff\xff\xff", ubuffer[2]);
             TJC.print(str);

           }

          }
        } else {
          TJC.read();       //         1
        }
    }
}

import machine
import time

#
FRAME_LENGTH=7

a=0
nowtime=0

#              0            115200
uart = machine.UART(0, baudrate=115200)

#      25            LED
led_pin = machine.Pin(25, machine.Pin.OUT)


#
def sendEnd():
    #
    hex_data = [0xff, 0xff, 0xff]

    #
    uart.write(bytearray(hex_data))


#
def tm0(timer):
    global a
    str = "n0.val={}".format(a)
    uart.write(str)
    sendEnd()

    str = "t0.txt=\"          {}\"".format(a)
    uart.write(str)
    sendEnd()


    str = "click b0,1"
    uart.write(str)
    sendEnd()

    time.sleep(0.05)

                                                                   (   )

   str = "click b0,0"
   uart.write(str)
   sendEnd()
   a+=1

#                  1

# ubuffer
ubuffer = []

while True:

   #                           ubuffer
   while uart.any():
       data = uart.read()
       if data:
           ubuffer.extend(data)

    #   ubuffer
    if len(ubuffer) >= FRAME_LENGTH:
        #
        if ubuffer[0] == 0x55 and ubuffer[4] == 0xff and ubuffer[5] == 0xff and␣
,→ubuffer[6] == 0xff:

            #             led
            if ubuffer[1] == 0x01:
                status = ""
                if ubuffer[3] == 0x01:
                    status = "on"
                    if ubuffer[2] == 0x00:
                        led_pin.value(1)
                else:
                    status = "off"
                    if ubuffer[2] == 0x00:
                        led_pin.value(0)
                str = "msg.txt=\"led {} is {}\"".format(ubuffer[2], status)
                uart.write(str)
                sendEnd()
            #                   h0
            elif ubuffer[1] == 0x02:
                str = "msg.txt=\"h0.val is {}\"".format(ubuffer[2])
                uart.write(str)
                sendEnd()
            #                   h1
            elif ubuffer[1] == 0x03:
                str = "msg.txt=\"h1.val is {}\"".format(ubuffer[2])
                uart.write(str)
                sendEnd()
            #     1
            del ubuffer[:FRAME_LENGTH]


       else:
           #              1
           del ubuffer[0]

import time
import psutil
import serial

time.sleep(3)

# function of Get CPU State;
def getCPUstate(interval=1):
    return (" CPU: " + str(psutil.cpu_percent(interval)) + "%")


# function of Get Memory
def getMemorystate():
    phymem = psutil.virtual_memory()
    line = "Memory: %5s%% %6s/%s" % (
        phymem.percent,
        str(int(phymem.used / 1024 / 1024)) + "M",
        str(int(phymem.total / 1024 / 1024)) + "M"
    )
    return line


def bytes2human(n):
    """
    >>> bytes2human(10000)
    '9.8 K'
    >>> bytes2human(100001221)
    '95.4 M'
    """
    symbols = ('K', 'M', 'G', 'T', 'P', 'E', 'Z', 'Y')
    prefix = {}
                                                                           (   )



                                                373
                                                                                       (      )
       for i, s in enumerate(symbols):
           prefix[s] = 1 << (i + 1) * 10
       for s in reversed(symbols):
           if n >= prefix[s]:
               value = float(n) / prefix[s]
               return '%.2f %s' % (value, s)
       return '%.2f B' % (n)


def poll(interval):
    """Retrieve raw stats within an interval window."""
    tot_before = psutil.net_io_counters()
    pnic_before = psutil.net_io_counters(pernic=True)
    # sleep some time
    time.sleep(interval)
    tot_after = psutil.net_io_counters()
    pnic_after = psutil.net_io_counters(pernic=True)
    # get cpu state
    cpu_state = getCPUstate(interval)
    # get memory
    memory_state = getMemorystate()
    return (tot_before, tot_after, pnic_before, pnic_after, cpu_state, memory_state)


def refresh_window(tot_before, tot_after, pnic_before, pnic_after, cpu_state, memory_
 ,→state):



       str = "t0.txt=\"" + time.asctime() + " | " + cpu_state + " | " + memory_state + "\""

       serial.write(str.encode("GB2312"))
       serial.write(bytes.fromhex('ff ff ff'))

       nic_names = pnic_after.keys()
       # nic_names.sort(key=lambda x: sum(pnic_after[x]), reverse=True)

       for name in nic_names:
           #                 ,        WLAN,          "        \       Internet\        "
           if name == "      ":
               stats_before = pnic_before[name]
               stats_after = pnic_after[name]
               templ = "%-15s %15s %15s"
               str = "t1.txt=\"            " + bytes2human(stats_after.bytes_sent) + " ␣
,→           :" + bytes2human(stats_after.bytes_sent - stats_before.bytes_sent) + '/s\"'
               serial.write(str.encode("GB2312"))
               serial.write(bytes.fromhex('ff ff ff'))
               str = "t2.txt=\"            " + bytes2human(stats_after.bytes_recv) + " ␣
,→           :" + bytes2human(stats_after.bytes_recv - stats_before.bytes_recv) + '/s\"'
               serial.write(str.encode("GB2312"))
               serial.write(bytes.fromhex('ff ff ff'))


try:
       interval = 0
       portx = "COM9"
       bauds = 115200
       timex = 5
       serial = serial.Serial(port=portx,baudrate=bauds,timeout=timex)
       while 1:
           args = poll(interval)
           refresh_window(*args)
           interval = 1

except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):

import requests

#       GNU / Linux    / dev / ttyUSB0       Windows     COM1
#                 2400 4800 9600 19200 38400 57600 115200 230400 256000 512000 921600
port="com9"
baud=115200

#
task1time=600
 ,→# 600
task2time=10         #   10

#
gold = 0
silver = 0
bronze = 0

url="https://api.mg-tool.cn/v1/jpb/?country=    "
def repeat_task1():
    response = requests.get(url)
    if response.status_code == 200:
        data = response.json() #                  JSON
        print(data)
        #
        china_medal_data = next(item for item in data['data'] if item['country'] == '
                                                                                    (   )



                                                   376
                                                                                            (   )
,→   ')

             #
             global gold,silver,bronze
             gold = china_medal_data['gold']
             silver = china_medal_data['silver']
             bronze = china_medal_data['bronze']

             print(f"               {gold}")
             print(f"               {silver}")
             print(f"               {bronze}")

          else:
              print("              ",response.status_code)

          Timer(task1time,repeat_task1).start()

def repeat_task2():
     try:
     #        ,None                0                                       (
     #

     #

 ,→8              utf8      usarthmi          GB2312              GB2312              GBK

         print("              "+str)

         print("              " + str)
         #

         ser.close()#

          except Exception as e:
              print("---    --- ",e)

          Timer(task2time, repeat_task2).start()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    repeat_task1()
    repeat_task2()

data0
data0.txt                       :aaaa^bbbb^cccc^dddd

spstr data0.txt,t1.txt,"^",1                          //   ^         ,        1   ,t0    "bbbb"
spstr data0.txt,t2.txt,"^",2                          //   ^         ,        2   ,t0    "cccc"
spstr data0.txt,t3.txt,"^",3                          //   ^         ,        3   ,t0    "dddd"

            t0.txt                    cccc

spstr ipAddress.txt,t1.txt,".",1                           //   .         ,        1    ,t1   "168"

spstr ipAddress.txt,t2.txt,".",2                           //   .         ,        2    ,t2   "1"

spstr ipAddress.txt,t3.txt,".",3                           //   .         ,        3    ,t3   "1"

if(p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].type==54)
{
  covx p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].val,input.txt,0,0
  inputlenth.val=24
}else if(p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].type==59)
{
  inputlenth.val=p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].val
  if(inputlenth.val<0)
  {
    inputlenth.val*=-1
    input.txt="-"
  }else
  {
    input.txt=""
  }
  temp.val=1
  for(temp2.val=0;temp2.val<p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].vvs1;temp2.val++)
  {
    temp.val*=10
  }
  temp2.val=inputlenth.val/temp.val//
  cov temp2.val,tempstr.txt,0
  input.txt+=tempstr.txt+"."
  temp2.val=temp2.val*temp.val-inputlenth.val//
  if(temp2.val<0)
  {
    temp2.val*=-1
  }
  covx temp2.val,tempstr.txt,p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].vvs1,0
  input.txt+=tempstr.txt
  inputlenth.val=24
}else
{
  input.txt=p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].txt
  inputlenth.val=p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].txt_maxl
  if(p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].type==116)
  {
    show.pw=p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].pw
  }
}
show.txt=input.txt

if(p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].type==54)
{
  covx input.txt,p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].val,0,0
}else if(p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].type==59)
{
  covx input.txt,temp.val,0,0
  if(temp.val<0)
  {
    temp.val*=-1
  }
  for(temp2.val=0;temp2.val<p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].vvs1;temp2.val++)
  {
    temp.val*=10
  }
  p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].val=temp.val
  strlen input.txt,temp.val
  temp.val--
  while(temp.val>=0)
  {
    substr input.txt,tempstr.txt,temp.val,1
    if(tempstr.txt==".")
    {
      substr input.txt,tempstr.txt,temp.val+1,p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].vvs1
      covx tempstr.txt,temp2.val,0,0
      strlen tempstr.txt,temp.val
      while(temp.val<p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].vvs1)
      {
        temp2.val*=10
        temp.val++
      }
      p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].val+=temp2.val
      temp.val=-1
    }
    temp.val--
  }
  substr input.txt,tempstr.txt,0,1
  if(tempstr.txt=="-")
  {
    p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].val*=-1
  }
}else
{
  p[loadpageid.val].b[loadcmpid.val].txt=input.txt
}

import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, filedialog
import serial
import serial.tools.list_ports
import threading
import time
import os
from datetime import datetime

class TJCDownloadTool(tk.Tk):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.title("                   ")
        self.geometry("800x600")

         #
         self.serial_port = None
         self.connect_baud = 0
         self.download_baud = 921600
         self.connecting = False
         self.downloading = False
         self.transferred = 0
                                                   (   )



                                            1011
                                                                                      (   )
       self.last_update = time.time()
       self.speed = 0

       #
       self.create_widgets()
       self.scan_serial_ports()

   def create_widgets(self):
       #
       ttk.Label(self, text="        :").place(x=20, y=20)
       self.port_combobox = ttk.Combobox(self, width=15)
       self.port_combobox.place(x=100, y=20)

       #
       ttk.Label(self, text="          :").place(x=20, y=60)
       self.baud_combobox = ttk.Combobox(self, width=15, values=[
           2400, 4800, 9600, 19200, 38400, 57600,
           115200, 230400, 256000, 512000, 921600
       ])
       self.baud_combobox.set(921600)
       self.baud_combobox.place(x=100, y=60)

       #
       self.file_btn = ttk.Button(self, text="        ", command=self.select_file)
       self.file_btn.place(x=20, y=100)
       self.file_path = ttk.Entry(self, width=50)
       self.file_path.place(x=100, y=100)

        #
        self.download_btn = ttk.Button(self, text="         ", command=self.start_
,→download)

        self.download_btn.place(x=20, y=140)

       #
       ttk.Label(self, text="        :").place(x=20, y=180)
       self.speed_label = ttk.Label(self, text="0 kB/s")
       self.speed_label.place(x=100, y=180)

        #
        self.progress = ttk.Progressbar(self, orient=tk.HORIZONTAL, length=750, mode=
,→'determinate')

        self.progress.place(x=20, y=220)

       #
       self.log_text = tk.Text(self, width=95, height=25)
       self.log_text.place(x=20, y=260)

    def scan_serial_ports(self):
        ports = sorted(serial.tools.list_ports.comports(), key=lambda p: p.device,␣
,→reverse=True)

        self.port_combobox['values'] = [p.device for p in ports]
        if ports:
            self.port_combobox.set(ports[0].device)

   def select_file(self):
       path = filedialog.askopenfilename(filetypes=[("TFT files", "*.tft")])
       if path:
           self.file_path.delete(0, tk.END)
           self.file_path.insert(0, path)

   def log(self, message):
                                                                                      (   )


                                          1012
                                                                                       (       )
           self.log_text.insert(tk.END, f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S')}] {message}\
,→   n")
           self.log_text.see(tk.END)

      def update_speed(self):
          if self.downloading:
              now = time.time()
              elapsed = now - self.last_update
              if elapsed > 0:
                  self.speed = (self.transferred / elapsed) / 1024
                  self.speed_label.config(text=f"{self.speed:.1f} kB/s")
              self.last_update = now
              self.transferred = 0
              self.after(1000, self.update_speed)

      def start_download(self):
          if not self.downloading:
              self.downloading = True
              self.transferred = 0
              self.last_update = time.time()
              self.download_btn.config(text="        ...")
              self.progress['value'] = 0
              self.after(1000, self.update_speed)
              threading.Thread(target=self.download_process).start()

      def download_process(self):
          port = self.port_combobox.get()
          file_path = self.file_path.get()

           if not port or not file_path:
               self.log("                     ")
               self.reset_btn()
               return

           try:
                  #
                  self.log("        ...")
                  connect_bauds = [9600, 115200, 19200, 38400, 57600,
                              230400, 256000, 512000, 921600, 4800, 2400]

            for baud in connect_bauds:
                self.log(f"      {baud}       ...")
                try:
                    with serial.Serial(port, baud, timeout=0.1) as ser:
                        connect_cmd = bytes.fromhex(
                             "44 52 41 4B 4A 48 53 55 59 44 47 42 4E 43 4A 48 47 4A 4B 53␣
,→48 42 44 4E FF FF FF 00 FF FF FF 63 6F 6E 6E 65 63 74 FF FF FF"

                        )
                        ser.write(connect_cmd)
                        time.sleep((1000000 / baud + 30) / 1000)
                        response = ser.read(1024)
                        if b'comok' in response:
                             self.connect_baud = baud
                             self.log(f"        !       : {baud}")
                             self.log(f"        : {response.decode(errors='ignore')}")
                             break
                except Exception as e:
                    continue

                  if not self.connect_baud:
                      self.log("        !")
                                                                                       (       )


                                                   1013
                                                                           (   )
               self.reset_btn()
               return

            #
            self.download_btn.config(text="        ...")
            self.send_download_command(port, file_path)

       except Exception as e:
           self.log(f"        : {str(e)}")
       finally:
           self.reset_btn()

   def send_download_command(self, port, file_path):
       try:
            #
            file_size = os.path.getsize(file_path)
            download_baud = int(self.baud_combobox.get())
            self.progress['maximum'] = file_size

            #     whmi-wri
            with serial.Serial(port, self.connect_baud) as ser:
                cmd = f"whmi-wri {file_size},{download_baud},0".encode()
                ser.write(cmd + b'\xff\xff\xff')
                time.sleep(0.35)

               #
               ser.baudrate = download_baud
               response = ser.read(1)
               if response != b'\x05':
                   self.log("                    ")
                   return

               #
               self.log("            ...")
               with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
                   while True:
                       chunk = f.read(4096)
                       if not chunk:
                           break
                       ser.write(chunk)
                       self.transferred += len(chunk)
                       self.progress['value'] += len(chunk)
                       #
                       while True:
                           resp = ser.read(1)
                           if resp == b'\x05':
                               break
               self.log("            !")

       except Exception as e:
           self.log(f"        : {str(e)}")

   def reset_btn(self):
       self.downloading = False
       self.connect_baud = 0
       self.download_btn.config(text="        ")
       self.progress['value'] = 0
       self.speed_label.config(text="0 kB/s")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    app = TJCDownloadTool()

      app.mainloop()

8.4.2 CRC16

//         MODBUS_CRC16

static U8 InvertUint8(U8 data)
{
   int i;
   U8 newtemp8 = 0;
   for (i = 0; i < 8; i++)
   {
      if ( (data & (1 << i) ) != 0) newtemp8 |= (U8)(1 << (7 - i));
   }
   return newtemp8;
}
static U16 InvertUint16(U16 data)
{
   int i;
   U16 newtemp16 = 0;
   for (i = 0; i < 16; i++)
   {
      if ( (data & (1 << i) ) != 0) newtemp16 |= (U16)(1 << (15 - i));
   }
   return newtemp16;
}
U16 CRC16_MODBUS(U8* data, int lenth)
{
   int i;
   U16 wCRCin = 0xFFFF;
   U16 wCPoly = 0x8005;
   U16 wChar = 0;
   while (lenth > 0)
   {
      wChar = *data;
      data++;
      wChar = InvertUint8( (U8)wChar);
      wCRCin ^= (U16)(wChar << 8);
                                                                             (   )


                                                      1016
                                                                                    (   )
          for (i = 0; i < 8; i++)
          {
             if ((wCRCin & 0x8000) != 0)
             {
                wCRCin = (U16)( (wCRCin << 1) ^ wCPoly);
             }else
             {
                wCRCin = (U16)(wCRCin << 1);
             }
          }
          lenth=lenth-1;
    }
    wCRCin = InvertUint16(wCRCin);
    return (wCRCin);
}

import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, filedialog, messagebox
import struct
import os

class DataToCsvConverter:
    def __init__(self, root):
        self.root = root
        self.root.title("Data             ")
        self.root.geometry("600x500")

         #
         self.file_path = tk.StringVar()
         self.output_path = tk.StringVar()
         self.encoding_var = tk.StringVar(value="GB2312")

         self.length_var = tk.StringVar(value="                   : ")
         self.dez_var = tk.StringVar(value="         : ")
         self.max_rows_var = tk.StringVar(value="         : ")
         self.actual_rows_var = tk.StringVar(value="         : ")

         self.create_widgets()

    def create_widgets(self):
        #
        encoding_frame = ttk.LabelFrame(self.root, text="         ", padding=10)
        encoding_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=5)

        ttk.Label(encoding_frame, text="        :").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="w")
        encoding_combo = ttk.Combobox(encoding_frame, textvariable=self.encoding_var,
                                    values=["GB2312", "utf-8", "GBK", "big5"], state=
,→"readonly")

        encoding_combo.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=5)
        encoding_frame.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)

         #
         file_frame = ttk.LabelFrame(self.root, text="        ", padding=10)
         file_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=5)

        ttk.Label(file_frame, text="        :").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="w")
        ttk.Entry(file_frame, textvariable=self.file_path, state="readonly").grid(row=0,␣
,→column=1, sticky="ew", padx=5)

        ttk.Button(file_frame, text="        ", command=self.select_file).grid(row=0,␣
,→column=2, padx=5)



        ttk.Label(file_frame, text="         :").grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="w")
        ttk.Entry(file_frame, textvariable=self.output_path, state="readonly").
,→grid(row=1, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=5)

        ttk.Button(file_frame, text="         ", command=self.select_output_path).
,→grid(row=1, column=2, padx=5)



         file_frame.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)

         #
         info_frame = ttk.LabelFrame(self.root, text="        ", padding=10)
         info_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=5)

   def select_file(self):
       """            """
       file_path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
           title="    .data    ",
           filetypes=[("Data files", "*.data"), ("All files", "*.*")]
       )
       if file_path:
           self.file_path.set(file_path)
           #
           output_path = os.path.splitext(file_path)[0] + ".csv"
           self.output_path.set(output_path)
           self.read_file_info()

   def select_output_path(self):
       """                """
       if self.file_path.get():
           default_name = os.path.splitext(self.file_path.get())[0] + ".csv"
       else:
           default_name = "output.csv"

       output_path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
           title="      CSV    ",
           defaultextension=".csv",
           filetypes=[("CSV files", "*.csv"), ("All files", "*.*")],
           initialfile=default_name
       )
       if output_path:
           self.output_path.set(output_path)

def try_decode(self, data, encoding):
    """                       """
    try:
         return data.decode(encoding), True
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
         return None, False

def convert_file(self):
    """            """
    if not self.file_path.get():
        messagebox.showwarning("          ", "                 ")
        return

    if not self.output_path.get():
        messagebox.showwarning("          ", "                      ")
        return

           self.log_message("              ...")

           with open(self.file_path.get(), 'rb') as f:

,→   {actual_rows}")

,→   newline='') as csv_file:

                              break

<script>
    //
    let fileData = null;
    let fileInfo = {
         recordLength: 0,
         fieldCount: 0,
         maxRows: 0,
         actualRows: 0
    };

    // DOM
    const browseBtn = document.getElementById('browseBtn');
    const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
    const inputFilePath = document.getElementById('inputFilePath');
    const outputFilePath = document.getElementById('outputFilePath');
    const encodingSelect = document.getElementById('encodingSelect');
    const convertBtn = document.getElementById('convertBtn');
    const statusEl = document.getElementById('status');

    //
    const recordLengthEl = document.getElementById('recordLength');
    const fieldCountEl = document.getElementById('fieldCount');
    const maxRowsEl = document.getElementById('maxRows');
    const actualRowsEl = document.getElementById('actualRows');

    //
    browseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        fileInput.click();
    });

    //
    fileInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (!file) return;

        //
        inputFilePath.value = file.name;

        //
        const outputName = file.name.replace(/\.data$/, '.csv');
        outputFilePath.value = outputName;

        try {
                                                                        (   )


                                       1060
                                                                 (   )
          //
          const reader = new FileReader();
          reader.onload = (event) => {
              fileData = new Uint8Array(event.target.result);
              //
              parseFileInfo();
              statusEl.textContent = "            ";
              statusEl.className = "status success";
          };
          reader.onerror = () => {
              throw new Error("            ");
          };
          reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
      } catch (error) {
          statusEl.textContent = `      ${error.message}`;
          statusEl.className = "status error";
          resetFileInfo();
      }
});

//
function parseFileInfo() {
    try {
        //                     0x020c      4
        fileInfo.recordLength = readUint32LE(0x020c);
        //           0x0214      4
        fileInfo.fieldCount = readUint32LE(0x0214);
        //           0x0218      4
        fileInfo.maxRows = readUint32LE(0x0218);
        //           0x0208      4
        fileInfo.actualRows = readUint32LE(0x0208);

          //
          recordLengthEl.textContent = fileInfo.recordLength;
          fieldCountEl.textContent = fileInfo.fieldCount;
          maxRowsEl.textContent = fileInfo.maxRows;
          actualRowsEl.textContent = fileInfo.actualRows;
      } catch (error) {
          statusEl.textContent = `          ${error.message}`;
          statusEl.className = "status error";
          resetFileInfo();
      }
}

//                 4
function readUint32LE(offset) {
    if (!fileData || offset + 4 > fileData.length) {
        throw new Error("                      ");
    }
    return (
        fileData[offset] +
        (fileData[offset + 1] << 8) +
        (fileData[offset + 2] << 16) +
        (fileData[offset + 3] << 24)
    );
}

//
function resetFileInfo() {
    fileInfo = {
        recordLength: 0,
                                                                 (   )


                                    1061
                                                                            (   )
          fieldCount: 0,
          maxRows: 0,
          actualRows: 0
      };
      recordLengthEl.textContent = "--";
      fieldCountEl.textContent = "--";
      maxRowsEl.textContent = "--";
      actualRowsEl.textContent = "--";
      fileData = null;
}

//
convertBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
    if (!fileData) {
        statusEl.textContent = "                     .data       ";
        statusEl.className = "status error";
        return;
    }

      if (!outputFilePath.value) {
          statusEl.textContent = "                       ";
          statusEl.className = "status error";
          return;
      }

      try {
          statusEl.textContent = "           ...";
          statusEl.className = "status";
          convertBtn.disabled = true;

          //
          await convertDataToCsv();

          statusEl.textContent = "                    ";
          statusEl.className = "status success";
      } catch (error) {
          statusEl.textContent = `          ${error.message}`;
          statusEl.className = "status error";
      } finally {
          convertBtn.disabled = false;
      }
});

//
async function convertDataToCsv() {
    const { recordLength, fieldCount, actualRows } = fileInfo;
    const encoding = encodingSelect.value;
    const firstRecordOffset = 0x0400;
    const fieldLength = recordLength / fieldCount;

      if (!Number.isInteger(fieldLength)) {
          throw new Error("                                           ");
      }

      //     CSV
      const csvLines = [];

      //
      for (let row = 0; row < actualRows; row++) {
          const rowData = [];
          //
                                                                            (   )


                                      1062
                                                                                         (      )
                     const recordOffset = firstRecordOffset + row * recordLength;

                     //
                     for (let field = 0; field < fieldCount; field++) {
                         const fieldOffset = recordOffset + field * fieldLength;
                         //                 \0
                         let fieldBytes = [];
                         for (let i = 0; i < fieldLength; i++) {
                             const byte = fileData[fieldOffset + i];
                             if (byte === 0x00) break; //                 '\0'
                             fieldBytes.push(byte);
                         }

                         //
                         let fieldStr = await decodeBytes(fieldBytes, encoding);
                         //     ^
                         fieldStr = fieldStr.replace(/\^/g, ',');
                         //
                         fieldStr = fieldStr.trim();

                         rowData.push(fieldStr);
                     }

                     //             CSV
                     csvLines.push(rowData.join(','));
                 }

                 //     CSV
                 const csvContent = csvLines.join('\n');
                 //
                 downloadFile(csvContent, outputFilePath.value, 'text/csv');
           }

           //
           async function decodeBytes(bytes, encoding) {
               //                    TextDecoder
               const encodingMap = {
                   'gb2312': 'gbk', //              gbk     gb2312
                   'utf-8': 'utf-8',
                   'gbk': 'gbk',
                   'big5': 'big5'
               };

                 const decoder = new TextDecoder(encodingMap[encoding] || encoding, { fatal:␣
,→   false });
                 return decoder.decode(new Uint8Array(bytes));
           }

           //
           function downloadFile(content, fileName, mimeType) {
               const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mimeType });
               const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
               const a = document.createElement('a');
               a.href = url;
               a.download = fileName;
               document.body.appendChild(a);
               a.click();
               setTimeout(() => {
                   document.body.removeChild(a);
                   URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
               }, 100);
                                                                                         (      )


                                               1063
                       (   )
        }

11.5.8         printf




void ScreenPrintf(const char *cmd, ...)
{
    uint8_t txt[30];                                //
    uint8_t *txt_p;                                 //
    memset(&txt,0,sizeof(txt));
    memset(&txt_p,0,sizeof(txt_p));
    va_list args;       //        va_list
    va_start(args,cmd); // args
    vsprintf((char *)txt, (const char *)cmd, args);
    va_end(args);
    txt_p = txt;
    while(*txt_p!='\0')
    {
        USART_SCREEN_write(*txt_p);//
        txt_p++;
    }
    USART_SCREEN_write(0xff);//
    USART_SCREEN_write(0xff);//
    USART_SCREEN_write(0xff);//
}
//      ScreenPrintf("n0.val=%d",MyData); //
